ID: v45-manual-ppm4-condition-the-ppm4-battery-before-first-use
Title: Condition the PPM4 battery before first use
Product: PPM4
Category: Battery / Power
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, battery / power, battery, conditioning, first use, charge, charger, MSS235, lithium polymer
Questions:
- How do I condition the battery before first use on the PPM4?
- How to condition the battery before first use PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I condition the battery before first use?
- PPM4 condition the battery before first use
- Where is condition the battery before first use on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to condition the battery before first use on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to condition the battery before first use on the PPM4?
- I need help with condition the battery before first use on PPM4
- How long does PPM4 take to charge?
- Can I use any charger for PPM4?
- How often should I charge unused PPM4?
Answer:
Before first use, the manual says to condition the internal rechargeable lithium polymer battery. Turn the unit on with the ON/OFF switch and let it operate in AC mode until the battery discharges completely; the screen and LED will go off when depleted. Then charge it to full, which takes about 7 hours. Use only the Abatement-supplied charger MSS235, because another power supply can damage the unit. Charge the battery every 6 months even if the unit is not being used.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-ppm4-ac-power-mode-versus-battery-mode
Title: PPM4 AC Power Mode versus Battery Mode
Product: PPM4
Category: Battery / Power
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, battery / power, battery mode, ac power mode, screen off, display, led, 3 days, 8 hours
Questions:
- How do I choose AC mode or Battery Mode on the PPM4?
- How to choose AC mode or Battery Mode PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I choose AC mode or Battery Mode?
- PPM4 choose AC mode or Battery Mode
- Where is choose AC mode or Battery Mode on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to choose AC mode or Battery Mode on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to choose AC mode or Battery Mode on the PPM4?
- I need help with choose AC mode or Battery Mode on PPM4
- Why did the PPM4 screen turn off?
- Why is the PPM4 LED flashing every 5 seconds?
- How long will PPM4 run on battery?
- How do I keep the PPM4 screen on?
Answer:
AC Power Mode is for continuous operation when the display should remain visible. With the charger connected, the display stays active and the LED stays on. Battery Mode is for unattended battery operation: after one minute of inactivity the display turns off, the front LED flashes every 5 seconds, and the display wakes on alarm or touch. Battery Mode can monitor for up to about 3 days from a full charge. If Battery Mode is selected but the charger is connected, the screen remains on because the unit knows it has power; if power is lost, it returns to normal Battery Mode behavior.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-turn-the-ppm4-on-off-or-wake-the-display
Title: Turn the PPM4 on, off, or wake the display
Product: PPM4
Category: Battery / Power
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, battery / power, on off switch, power button, wake screen, display, led, turn off
Questions:
- How do I turn the PPM4 on or off on the PPM4?
- How to turn the PPM4 on or off PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I turn the PPM4 on or off?
- PPM4 turn the PPM4 on or off
- Where is turn the PPM4 on or off on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to turn the PPM4 on or off on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to turn the PPM4 on or off on the PPM4?
- I need help with turn the PPM4 on or off on PPM4
- How do I wake up the PPM4 screen?
- How do I shut down PPM4?
- How long do I hold the PPM4 power button?
Answer:
Short-press the ON/OFF button to activate or wake the display. Hold the ON/OFF button for about 3 seconds to turn the main power on or off. When power is on, the front LED is on. When the unit is turned off by holding the ON/OFF button, the LED remains off and battery life is maximized because the unit is not recording or monitoring pressure.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-use-the-ppm4-power-jack-and-charger
Title: Use the PPM4 power jack and charger
Product: PPM4
Category: Battery / Power
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, battery / power, power jack, charger, charging, battery, power supply, operate indefinitely
Questions:
- How do I charge or power the PPM4 on the PPM4?
- How to charge or power the PPM4 PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I charge or power the PPM4?
- PPM4 charge or power the PPM4
- Where is charge or power the PPM4 on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to charge or power the PPM4 on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to charge or power the PPM4 on the PPM4?

Answer:
Plug the supplied battery charger into the PPM4 power jack. Charging is automatic while the charger is connected and powered. The unit can operate indefinitely with the charger connected. The battery charges whether the unit is switched on or off, as long as the charger is connected and powered.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-connect-ppm4-room-and-ref-tubing
Title: Connect PPM4 ROOM and REF tubing
Product: PPM4
Category: Tubing / Setup
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, tubing / setup, room port, ref port, reference, tubing, hose, quick disconnect, pressure ports
Questions:
- How do I connect pressure tubing on the PPM4?
- How to connect pressure tubing PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I connect pressure tubing?
- PPM4 connect pressure tubing
- Where is connect pressure tubing on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to connect pressure tubing on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to connect pressure tubing on the PPM4?
- I need help with connect pressure tubing on PPM4
- Which PPM4 port goes in the room?
- What does REF mean on PPM4?
- Do I need tubing on the REF port?
Answer:
The PPM4 has two pressure input ports: REF and ROOM. The REF port is the reference pressure and its tubing should go to an area outside the monitored space. If the PPM4 is already outside the monitored room, no hose is needed on the REF input. The tubing from the room being monitored connects to the ROOM port. Use the supplied quick disconnects and 1/8 in tubing.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-install-ppm4-inline-filters-correctly
Title: Install PPM4 inline filters correctly
Product: PPM4
Category: Tubing / Setup
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, tubing / setup, inline filters, filter arrow, dust, moisture, replace filters, delayed readings
Questions:
- How do I use inline filters on PPM4 tubing on the PPM4?
- How to use inline filters on PPM4 tubing PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I use inline filters on PPM4 tubing?
- PPM4 use inline filters on PPM4 tubing
- Where is use inline filters on PPM4 tubing on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to use inline filters on PPM4 tubing on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to use inline filters on PPM4 tubing on the PPM4?
- I need help with use inline filters on PPM4 tubing on PPM4
- Which way does the PPM4 filter arrow point?
- How often do I replace PPM4 filters?
- Do I need filters on PPM4 tubing?
Answer:
Use the inline filters at all times while the PPM4 is operating to help keep dust and moisture from damaging the internal sensors. Place the filters inline with each input tube in a convenient location. The arrow on each filter must point toward the monitor. Filters should be replaced annually in very dirty environments; in normal clean indoor environments they may be used up to 5 years. Using a non-specified filter can cause delayed or incorrect differential pressure readings.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-operate-the-ppm4-with-the-case-closed
Title: Operate the PPM4 with the case closed
Product: PPM4
Category: Tubing / Setup
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, tubing / setup, case closed, right angle adapter, closed box, slots, pinched tubing, lid
Questions:
- How do I run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed on the PPM4?
- How to run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed?
- PPM4 run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed
- Where is run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed on the PPM4?
- I need help with run tubing with the PPM4 lid closed on PPM4
- Can I close the PPM4 case while monitoring?
- How do I route PPM4 tubing with the lid closed?
Answer:
For closed-box operation, use the right-angle adapters and pass tubing and cables through the slots in the base of the case. Be careful when closing the lid so tubing and cables are not pinched; they must pass through their allotted slots. The manual specifically warns that pinched tubing/cables can occur if the lid is closed carelessly.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-do-not-blow-into-ppm4-ports-or-tubing
Title: Do not blow into PPM4 ports or tubing
Product: PPM4
Category: Tubing / Setup
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, tubing / setup, do not blow, pressure sensor damage, ports, hose, sensor safety
Questions:
- How do I avoid damaging the pressure sensor on the PPM4?
- How to avoid damaging the pressure sensor PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I avoid damaging the pressure sensor?
- PPM4 avoid damaging the pressure sensor
- Where is avoid damaging the pressure sensor on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to avoid damaging the pressure sensor on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to avoid damaging the pressure sensor on the PPM4?
- I need help with avoid damaging the pressure sensor on PPM4
- Can I blow into the PPM4 tube to test it?
- Will blowing into PPM4 damage it?
Answer:
Do not blow into the PPM4 hose or pressure ports. The manual warns that doing so may permanently damage the pressure sensor. For troubleshooting, use proper tubing checks, calibration steps, and filter inspection instead of blowing into the ports.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-silence-or-stop-a-ppm4-alarm
Title: Silence or stop a PPM4 alarm
Product: PPM4
Category: Alarms
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, alarms, stop alarm, silence alarm, mute alarm, alarm red screen, external alarms
Questions:
- How do I silence the alarm on the PPM4?
- How to silence the alarm PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I silence the alarm?
- PPM4 silence the alarm
- Where is silence the alarm on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to silence the alarm on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to silence the alarm on the PPM4?
- I need help with silence the alarm on PPM4
- How do I stop PPM4 beeping?
- Why is PPM4 still red after stop alarm?
- Does STOP ALARM clear the alarm condition?
Answer:
When the PPM4 is in alarm, open the main menu with the gear icon and press STOP ALARM. This silences the audible alarm and, if connected, external alarms through the web or mobile app. The main screen remains red until the differential pressure returns within the user-specified limit, so STOP ALARM silences the alarm but does not clear the out-of-range condition.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-set-ppm4-pressure-alarm-limits
Title: Set PPM4 pressure alarm limits
Product: PPM4
Category: Alarms
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, alarms, alarm triggers, pressure limit, negative room, positive room, threshold, sensor settings
Questions:
- How do I set pressure alarm limits on the PPM4?
- How to set pressure alarm limits PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I set pressure alarm limits?
- PPM4 set pressure alarm limits
- Where is set pressure alarm limits on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to set pressure alarm limits on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to set pressure alarm limits on the PPM4?
- I need help with set pressure alarm limits on PPM4
- How do I set PPM4 negative pressure alarm?
- How do I set PPM4 positive pressure alarm?
- What is the PPM4 alarm trigger range?
Answer:
Go to Room Settings → Sensor Settings → Alarm Triggers. The alarm point sets the acceptable pressure range. For a negative room, anything above the limit alarms. For a positive room, anything below the limit alarms. The manual gives a range of -9.960 Pa to 9.960 Pa (-0.0400 to 0.0400 inWC) with a factory default of 0.0000 inWC.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-download-ppm4-history-to-usb

Questions:
- How do I download the archive to USB on the PPM4?

Product: PPM4
Category: Maintenance
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true

- What is the menu path to clean the display on PPM4?

- I need help with clean the display on PPM4

Turn the system off so dirt and oil are easier to see on the black screen. Use a soft, damp cloth to gently wipe the TFT display horizontally or vertically. ID: v45-manual-ppm4-connect-ppm4-auxiliary-sensors

Product: PPM4
Category: External Sensors
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true

Connect the auxiliary sensor to the PPM4 main unit using an Ethernet cable. Make sure the cable is fully seated in both the sensor and main unit. ID: v45-manual-ppm4-set-ppm4-external-sensor-dip-switches
Title: Set PPM4 external sensor DIP switches
Product: PPM4
Category: External Sensors
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, external sensors, DIP switches, DIP 1, DIP 4, sensor number, room number, Room 1 Sensor 1, Room 2 Sensor 2
Questions:
- How do I set sensor DIP switches on the PPM4?
- How to set sensor DIP switches PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I set sensor DIP switches?
- PPM4 set sensor DIP switches
- Where is set sensor DIP switches on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to set sensor DIP switches on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to set sensor DIP switches on the PPM4?
- I need help with set sensor DIP switches on PPM4
- What do DIP switches do on PPM4 sensor?
- How do I assign PPM4 sensor to room 2?
Answer:
Before enabling an external sensor in software, set the sensor DIP switches for the room and sensor number. DIP 1 sets Sensor 1 or Sensor 2: DIP 1 OFF = Sensor 1 and DIP 1 ON = Sensor 2. DIP 4 sets room: DIP 4 OFF = Room 1 and DIP 4 ON = Room 2. The resulting combinations are Room 1 Sensor 1: DIP 1 OFF/DIP 4 OFF; Room 1 Sensor 2: DIP 1 ON/DIP 4 OFF; Room 2 Sensor 1: DIP 1 OFF/DIP 4 ON; Room 2 Sensor 2: DIP 1 ON/DIP 4 ON.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-enable-a-ppm4-external-sensor-in-software
Title: Enable a PPM4 external sensor in software
Product: PPM4
Category: External Sensors
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, external sensors, sensor enable, sensor settings, enable external sensor, DIP 1 off, DIP 1 on
Questions:
- How do I enable a sensor on the PPM4?
- How to enable a sensor PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I enable a sensor?
- PPM4 enable a sensor
- Where is enable a sensor on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to enable a sensor on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to enable a sensor on the PPM4?
- I need help with enable a sensor on PPM4
- Why is my PPM4 sensor not showing?
- Where do I enable PPM4 sensor?
Answer:
After connecting the sensor and setting DIP switches, go to Room Settings → select Room 1 or Room 2 → Sensor Settings → Sensor Enable. Enable the correct sensor number. Enable Sensor 1 if DIP 1 is OFF. Enable Sensor 2 if DIP 1 is ON. Save or confirm the setting, then check that the sensor appears active in the room menu or sensor status screen.

Non-warranty repairs require customer approval and a purchase order before repair charges are made.

- I need help with find PPM4 specifications on PPM4
- What is PPM4 pressure range?
- What is PPM4 accuracy?
- How heavy is PPM4?
- What size is PPM4?
Answer:
Key PPM4 specifications from the manual: measurement range ±0.10 inWC or ±25 Pa; accuracy ±3% full scale; display resolution 0.0001 inWC or 0.025 Pa; custom alarm setpoints from -0.0400 to 0.0400 inWC (-9.964 to 9.964 Pa); 7 in 800 x 480 color display; operating temperature 41°F to 131°F (5°C to 55°C); 0 to 85% RH non-condensing; 9000 ft maximum operating altitude; USB V2.0 Type A; 2.4 GHz Wi-Fi; physical size 12 in L x 10 in W x 5 in H; weight about 5 lb including case, charger, and hose.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-what-ppm4-stores-in-the-archive
Title: What PPM4 stores in the archive
Product: PPM4
Category: Data Logging
Source: PPM4 Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/16K9WVfojveqaZWyiTfti14W0QO2rAuhv/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: ppm4, data logging, archive, logged events, non-volatile memory, job number, alarm condition, low battery, configuration change
Questions:
- How do I understand the data log on the PPM4?
- How to understand the data log PPM4?
- PPM4 how do I understand the data log?
- PPM4 understand the data log
- Where is understand the data log on the PPM4?
- What is the menu path to understand the data log on PPM4?
- Can you walk me through how to understand the data log on the PPM4?
- I need help with understand the data log on PPM4
- What events does PPM4 log?
- How many events can PPM4 store?
- Does PPM4 log configuration changes?
Answer:
The PPM4 stores more than 12,000 logged events in non-volatile memory. Logged events include alarm condition with current pressure, system startup, alarm reset, configuration change, low battery, archive erased, calibration, start Battery Mode, start ON Mode, start OFF Mode, pressure interval, and next day. Date and time are shown with logged events. Job numbers are alphanumeric with 8-digit length and help distinguish job sites.

Answer:
The RPM is a wall-mounted room pressure monitor. The manual describes real-time differential pressure monitoring for up to two rooms, a built-in pressure sensor for primary room monitoring, support for up to 10 daisy-chained external sensors via RJ45 Ethernet, a 7 in color touchscreen, audible/visual alarms, optional email/text alarm notifications, selectable pressure/environmental units, USB firmware/data functions, optional cloud connectivity, and AC-powered operation.

Product: RPM
Category: Connections
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true

Questions:
- How do I avoid connecting to a network switch on the RPM?
- How to avoid connecting to a network switch RPM?
- RPM how do I avoid connecting to a network switch?
- RPM avoid connecting to a network switch
- Where is avoid connecting to a network switch on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to avoid connecting to a network switch on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to avoid connecting to a network switch on the RPM?
- I need help with avoid connecting to a network switch on RPM
- Can I plug RPM into a network switch?

- Can RPM sensors go through a router?
Answer:
Do not connect any RPM or sensor unit to a standard Ethernet network switch or router. The manual warns this may damage both the RPM and the network equipment. The RJ45 ports are for the RPM sensor bus, not a standard network connection.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-set-rpm-sensor-dip-switches
Title: Set RPM sensor DIP switches
Product: RPM
Category: Sensors
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, sensors, DIP switches, DIP 4, DIP 1, room select, sensor address, Room 1 Sensor 2, Room 2 Sensor 1
Questions:
- How do I set DIP switches on the RPM?
- How to set DIP switches RPM?
- RPM how do I set DIP switches?
- RPM set DIP switches
- Where is set DIP switches on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to set DIP switches on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to set DIP switches on the RPM?
- I need help with set DIP switches on RPM
- What DIP settings for RPM Room 2 Sensor 1?
- What DIP settings for RPM Room 1 Sensor 2?
- How do I assign sensors on RPM?
Answer:
Use DIP 4 for room select and DIP 1 for sensor address. DIP 4 OFF / DIP 1 OFF = Room 1 Sensor 1, but Room 1 Sensor 1 is reserved for the built-in pressure sensor. DIP 4 OFF / DIP 1 ON = Room 1 Sensor 2. DIP 4 ON / DIP 1 OFF = Room 2 Sensor 1. DIP 4 ON / DIP 1 ON = Room 2 Sensor 2.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-connect-rpm-external-sensors
Title: Connect RPM external sensors
Product: RPM
Category: Connections
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, connections, connect sensors, cat5e, straight-through ethernet, 50 feet, daisy chain, RJ45 port, sensor bus
Questions:
- How do I connect sensors on the RPM?
- How to connect sensors RPM?
- RPM how do I connect sensors?
- RPM connect sensors
- Where is connect sensors on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to connect sensors on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to connect sensors on the RPM?
- I need help with connect sensors on RPM
- What cable do RPM sensors use?
- How far can RPM sensor cables run?
- Does it matter which RPM RJ45 port I use?
Answer:
Use standard straight-through Ethernet cables, Cat5e or higher. Maximum total cable distance from the RPM to the last sensor should not exceed 50 ft. Connect an Ethernet cable from either RJ45 port on the RPM to the first sensor, then daisy-chain additional sensors from one sensor to the next. All sensors share the same bus regardless of which RPM RJ45 port is used.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-enable-rpm-sensors-in-the-user-interface
Title: Enable RPM sensors in the user interface
Product: RPM
Category: Sensors
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true

- Where do I enable RPM sensors?
- Why does RPM show communication error?

Answer:
To enable sensors for Room 1, go to Menu → Room Settings → Room 1 → Sensor Settings → Sensor Enable. Enable the sensors that match the DIP switch setup, select Submit, then confirm changes. Product: RPM
Category: Sensors
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, sensors, sensor settings, sensor enable, sensor alarm status, alarm triggers, enable disable sensors
Questions:
- How do I use sensor settings on the RPM?
- How to use sensor settings RPM?
- RPM how do I use sensor settings?
- RPM use sensor settings
- Where is use sensor settings on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to use sensor settings on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to use sensor settings on the RPM?
- I need help with use sensor settings on RPM
- What is RPM Sensor Settings for?
- How do I configure RPM sensors?
Answer:
Room Settings → Sensor Settings lets the user enable/disable sensors, enable/disable alarms, and set alarm point values. Use Sensor Enable to turn connected sensors on, Sensor Alarm Status to activate alarms for enabled sensors, and Alarm Triggers to set high/low or pressure limits.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-set-rpm-pressure-alarm-behavior-for-negative-or-positive-rooms
Title: Set RPM pressure alarm behavior for negative or positive rooms
Product: RPM
Category: Alarms
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, alarms, negative room, positive room, alarm point, above limit, below limit, alarm trigger range
Questions:
- How do I set pressure alarm points on the RPM?
- How to set pressure alarm points RPM?
- RPM how do I set pressure alarm points?
- RPM set pressure alarm points
- Where is set pressure alarm points on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to set pressure alarm points on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to set pressure alarm points on the RPM?
- I need help with set pressure alarm points on RPM
- How do RPM negative pressure alarms work?
- How do RPM positive pressure alarms work?
- What is RPM pressure alarm range?
Answer:
In Sensor Settings → Alarm Triggers, set the alarm point values. For a negative room, anything above the limit will alarm. For a positive room, anything below the limit will alarm. The manual gives the alarm-point range as -9.960 Pa to 9.960 Pa (-0.0400 to 0.0400 inWC), with factory default 0.0000 inWC.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-download-rpm-history-to-usb
Title: Download RPM history to USB
Product: RPM
Category: Data Logging
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, data logging, usb download, archive.csv, history archive, data log, csv, flash drive, inWC
Questions:
- How do I download archive to USB on the RPM?
- How to download archive to USB RPM?
- RPM how do I download archive to USB?
- RPM download archive to USB
- Where is download archive to USB on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to download archive to USB on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to download archive to USB on the RPM?
- I need help with download archive to USB on RPM
- How do I get data off RPM?
- What file does RPM export?
- Does RPM USB download erase history?
Answer:
Go to Data Log → USB Download. Insert the USB flash drive when prompted. Downloading history does not destroy internal records. Do not remove the USB until download is complete. The history downloads as ARCHIVE.CSV and can be opened in a text editor or spreadsheet. Pressures in the file are displayed in inWC units. Use only the supplied USB flash drive for this function.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-create-the-rpm-device-in-the-web-portal

Product: RPM
Category: Abatement Link / AT Connect

ID: v45-manual-rpm-verify-the-rpm-in-the-web-portal
Title: Verify the RPM in the web portal
Product: RPM
Category: Abatement Link / AT Connect
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, abatement link / at connect, verify device, verified devices, cloud setup, wifi, cellular, login credentials
Questions:
- How do I verify the RPM device on the RPM?
- How to verify the RPM device RPM?
- RPM how do I verify the RPM device?
- RPM verify the RPM device
- Where is verify the RPM device on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to verify the RPM device on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to verify the RPM device on the RPM?
- I need help with verify the RPM device on RPM
- How long does RPM verification take?
- How do I move RPM from non verified to verified?

First confirm the RPM is connected to Wi-Fi or cellular. On the RPM, go to Communications → Cloud Setup and enter the same credentials used for the web application. If the credentials are correct, the monitor gives an acceptance message. After about two minutes, the device should move to Verified Devices in the web application.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-replace-rpm-inline-filters
Title: Replace RPM inline filters
Product: RPM
Category: Maintenance
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: rpm, maintenance, filter replacement, inline filters, dust, moisture, 5 years
Questions:
- How do I replace filters on the RPM?
- How to replace filters RPM?
- RPM how do I replace filters?
- RPM replace filters
- Where is replace filters on the RPM?
- What is the menu path to replace filters on RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to replace filters on the RPM?
- I need help with replace filters on RPM
- How often do RPM filters need changing?
- What maintenance does RPM need?
Answer:
RPM maintenance consists of zero calibration as recommended and replacement of external filters. The inline filters should be used at all times to help keep dust and moisture from damaging internal sensors. Replace them annually in very dirty environments; in normal clean indoor environments, they may be used up to 5 years. Using filters not specified by the manufacturer can delay or distort differential pressure readings.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-clean-the-rpm-touchscreen-display
Title: Clean the RPM touchscreen display
Product: RPM
Category: Maintenance
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true

Turn the system off so dirt and oil are visible on the black screen. Use a soft, damp cloth to gently wipe the TFT display horizontally or vertically, then wipe away remaining moisture with a dry cloth. Do not use chemical cleaners because they may permanently damage the display.

Answer:
Abatement Technologies provides in-house calibration and repair for pressure monitors purchased from ATI. Calibrations are traceable to NIST and DAKKS calibration is available. Answer:
Key RPM specifications from the manual include: measurement range ±0.10 inWC or ±25 Pa; accuracy ±3% full scale; display resolution 0.0001 inWC or 0.025 Pa; alarm setpoints from -0.0400 to 0.0400 inWC (-9.964 to 9.964 Pa); audible internal speaker rated 85 dB at 10 cm; yellow pre-alarm and red alarm display warnings; quick-connect pressure ports adaptable to 1/8 in ID silicone tubing; operating temperature 41°F to 131°F (5°C to 55°C); storage temperature -22°F to 176°F (-30°C to 80°C); 0–85% RH non-condensing; 9000 ft maximum operating altitude; USB V2.0 Type A; 2.4 GHz Wi-Fi; physical dimensions 12 in L x 10 in W x 5 in H; approximate weight 5 lb including case, battery charger, and hose.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-ppm4-vs-rpm-basic-difference
Title: PPM4 vs RPM basic difference
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Manual How-To
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /products
Keywords: ppm4/rpm, manual how-to, PPM4 vs RPM, compare, portable, wall mounted, dual room, external sensors
Questions:
- How do I compare PPM4 and RPM on the PPM4/RPM?
- How to compare PPM4 and RPM PPM4/RPM?
- PPM4/RPM how do I compare PPM4 and RPM?
- PPM4/RPM compare PPM4 and RPM
- Where is compare PPM4 and RPM on the PPM4/RPM?
- What is the menu path to compare PPM4 and RPM on PPM4/RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to compare PPM4 and RPM on the PPM4/RPM?
- I need help with compare PPM4 and RPM on PPM4/RPM
- Should I use PPM4 or RPM?
- What is the difference between PPM4 and RPM?
- Is RPM portable like PPM4?
Answer:
Use the PPM4 as the portable pressure monitor: rugged case, battery/AC operation, and portable job-site pressure monitoring. Use the RPM as the wall-mounted room pressure monitor: AC powered, built-in pressure sensor for primary room monitoring, dual-room support, and support for external daisy-chained temperature, humidity, particle, and airflow sensors. Both use similar menu concepts for room settings, preferences, communications, data logs, administration, cloud setup, and USB exports.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-common-ppm4-rpm-pressure-alarm-setup
Title: Common PPM4/RPM pressure alarm setup
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Manual How-To
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /products
Keywords: ppm4/rpm, manual how-to, pressure alarms, alarm triggers, negative pressure, positive pressure, occupied, sensor alarm status
Questions:
- How do I set pressure alarms on either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- How to set pressure alarms on either monitor PPM4/RPM?
- PPM4/RPM how do I set pressure alarms on either monitor?
- PPM4/RPM set pressure alarms on either monitor
- Where is set pressure alarms on either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- What is the menu path to set pressure alarms on either monitor on PPM4/RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to set pressure alarms on either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- I need help with set pressure alarms on either monitor on PPM4/RPM
- How do I set negative pressure alarm?
- How do I set positive pressure alarm?
- Why are pressure alarms not working?
Answer:
For both PPM4 and RPM, pressure alarm setup follows the same concept: go to Room Settings → selected room → Sensor Settings → Alarm Triggers. Set the alarm point for the acceptable pressure limit. For negative pressure, readings above the limit alarm. For positive pressure, readings below the limit alarm. Then make sure the room is Occupied/alarms active and sensor alarm status is enabled if the alarm is sensor-specific.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-common-ppm4-rpm-usb-export-steps
Title: Common PPM4/RPM USB export steps
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Manual How-To
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /downloads
Keywords: ppm4/rpm, manual how-to, usb export, download data, archive.csv, history, data log, delete log
Questions:
- How do I download data from either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- How to download data from either monitor PPM4/RPM?
- PPM4/RPM how do I download data from either monitor?
- PPM4/RPM download data from either monitor
- Where is download data from either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- What is the menu path to download data from either monitor on PPM4/RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to download data from either monitor on the PPM4/RPM?
- I need help with download data from either monitor on PPM4/RPM
- How do I export monitor history?
- How do I download pressure logs?
- Does USB export erase data?
Answer:
For both monitors, use Data Log → USB Download. Insert the supplied USB flash drive when prompted and do not remove it until the download is complete. The history exports as ARCHIVE.CSV and can be opened in a text editor or spreadsheet. Downloading does not erase internal records. Use Data Log → Delete Log only when you intentionally want to erase the archive; the monitor records that the archive was erased.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-common-ppm4-rpm-cloud-setup-steps
Title: Common PPM4/RPM cloud setup steps
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Manual How-To
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | https://connect.abatement.com
Keywords: ppm4/rpm, manual how-to, cloud setup, Abatement Link, AT Connect, verify device, non-verified, verified devices, credentials
Questions:
- How do I connect either monitor to Abatement Link on the PPM4/RPM?
- How to connect either monitor to Abatement Link PPM4/RPM?
- PPM4/RPM how do I connect either monitor to Abatement Link?
- PPM4/RPM connect either monitor to Abatement Link
- Where is connect either monitor to Abatement Link on the PPM4/RPM?
- What is the menu path to connect either monitor to Abatement Link on PPM4/RPM?
- Can you walk me through how to connect either monitor to Abatement Link on the PPM4/RPM?

- How do I verify a monitor in Abatement Link?

Answer:
For PPM4 or RPM cloud setup, first create the device in the Abatement Connect web portal with the correct serial number and device type. Then connect the monitor to Wi-Fi or cellular. On the monitor, go to Communications → Cloud Setup → Login Info and enter the same web-portal credentials. If accepted, wait about two minutes for the device to move from Non-Verified to Verified Devices.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-how-to-clean-the-display
Title: How to clean the PPM4 or RPM display
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Maintenance
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /downloads
Keywords: display, screen, clean, cleaning, touchscreen, TFT, chemical cleaners, damp cloth, PPM4, RPM
Questions:
- How do I clean the display?
- How do I clean the screen?
- How do I clean the touchscreen?
- Can I use cleaner on the display?
- How do I clean PPM4 screen?
- How do I clean RPM screen?
- What should I use to clean the monitor display?
Answer:
For either PPM4 or RPM, turn the system off so dirt and oil are visible on the black screen. Use a soft, damp cloth to gently wipe the TFT display horizontally or vertically, then use a dry cloth to remove remaining moisture. Do not use chemical cleaners; the manuals warn chemical cleaners may permanently damage the display.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-how-to-update-firmware-from-usb
Title: How to update PPM4 or RPM firmware from USB
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Firmware
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /firmware
Keywords: firmware, update firmware, USB, correct files, 3 to 5 minutes, system menu, PPM4, RPM
Questions:
- How do I update firmware from USB?
- How do I update monitor firmware?
- How do I update PPM4 firmware?
- How do I update RPM firmware?
- Why does the firmware update button not appear?
- How long does firmware update take?
Answer:
On either PPM4 or RPM, go to Administration → System → Update Firmware. Insert a USB drive containing the correct firmware files provided by Abatement Technologies. When the correct file is detected, an update button appears. Wait about 3 to 5 minutes for the update to complete. For PPM4, also follow any version-specific migration instructions supplied with the firmware package.

ID: v45-manual-ppm4-rpm-how-to-connect-pressure-tubing
Title: How to connect PPM4 or RPM pressure tubing
Product: PPM4/RPM
Category: Tubing / Setup
Source: PPM4 and RPM Owner Manuals | /downloads
Keywords: tubing, ROOM port, REF port, reference, inline filters, filter arrow, pressure ports, PPM4, RPM
Questions:
- How do I connect pressure tubing?
- How do I connect tubing?
- Which tube goes to ROOM?
- Which tube goes to REF?
- What does REF mean?
- Do I need reference tubing?
- Which way do the filter arrows point?
Answer:
For PPM4 or RPM, connect the tubing from the monitored room to the ROOM port. The REF port is the reference pressure and should reference the area outside the monitored space. If the monitor itself is located outside the monitored room, the PPM4 manual says no hose is needed on the reference input. Use the supplied quick disconnects and put inline filters in the input tubing with the filter arrows pointing toward the monitor. Do not blow into the hose or ports.

ID: v45-manual-rpm-sensor-not-showing-natural
Title: RPM sensor not showing or no sensor values
Product: RPM
Category: Troubleshooting
Source: RPM Owner's Manual | https://docs.google.com/document/d/1fQubA6975M3K3Zy80_ylF3fbROo8CGRp/edit?usp=drive_link&ouid=106548179376433005372&rtpof=true&sd=true
Keywords: RPM sensor not showing, sensor values missing, communication error, DIP switch, ethernet cable, left RJ45, sensor enable
Questions:
- Why is my RPM sensor not showing?
- Why are RPM sensor values missing?
- RPM sensor not showing
- RPM sensor does not appear
- How do I fix RPM sensor communication error?
- Why does RPM have communication error alarm?
Answer:
If an RPM sensor does not show values, check the DIP switch assignment, confirm the physical Ethernet connections, make sure the sensor is powered, and verify the matching sensor is enabled in Menu → Room Settings → Room 1 or Room 2 → Sensor Settings → Sensor Enable. Do not enable a sensor that is not physically connected or the RPM can generate a communication error alarm. If values still do not appear, power cycle the affected sensor by unplugging and reconnecting its Ethernet cable. The manual also notes some setups may require daisy-chaining left RJ45 to left RJ45 for proper detection.
